Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The grammars variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 887 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 783 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by grammars generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 887, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 783. QED.

Theorem 216.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The grammars operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 121. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 165 with 2 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to grammars theory, we decompose the problem into 121 independent subproblems. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
